# Segment 134 — Test Case Cards

One card per test case (QE feedback Def_SepRun07/Def_SepRun10). Derived from the canonical JSON on every run; JSON stays authoritative. Fields flagged UNTRUSTED_VALID_VALUES, AMBIGUOUS_LENGTH or DELIBERATE_CONSTRAINT_VIOLATION are marked ⚠.

## TC-021029 — Business_Rule — Financial Transaction Request — When 'N' is sent, transaction should not be routed to credit and shoul

## TC-021103 — Business_Rule — Financial Transaction Request — Recurring MIT transaction must be identified as 'R' (Recurring).
